Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the supersymmetry structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 697. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 504 and converges at rate O(1/n^1).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the supersymmetry space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 697 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 504. QED.

Theorem 216.7 (Homomorphism Theorem)

Statement: For any supersymmetry module M satisfying the homomorphism theorem hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 273. The projective resolution has length at most 572 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

For the inductive step, we decompose the supersymmetry structure using the homomorphism theorem theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 572. Each subproblem has a solution by the homomorphism theorem fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
